# Wrist Arthritis

*Hand · 100 / 103*

## 1 Basics

### Patterns by location

| Pattern | Rank / key fact |
|---|---|
| **SLAC** | **Most common** |
| **STT arthrosis** | **2nd most common** |
| SNAC | Scaphoid nonunion |
| DRUJ arthrosis | Post-traumatic, RA |
| Pisotriquetral arthrosis | Ulnar-sided pain |

**Causes** — primary OA · **post-traumatic → SLAC/SNAC/DRUJ** · RA · congenital (**Madelung**) · idiopathic (**Kienböck, Preiser**)

## 2 Mechanism

**SLAC** — SL tear → **palmar rotary subluxation of scaphoid** → radiocarpal OA → **capitolunate** OA; **radiolunate spared**

**SNAC** — proximal pole stays with lunate, distal scaphoid flexes → early **radial styloid–distal scaphoid** OA; radiolunate spared

**RA wrist** — **supinated, palmarly dislocated, radially deviated, ulnarly translocated**; early DRUJ loss → **caput ulnae** (dorsal ulna)

### Golf – trailing-wrist dorsal rim impaction

| Wrist (TO) | Motion | Injuries |
|---|---|---|
| **Trailing** | Extends in backswing, flexes in follow-through | **Dorsal rim impaction syndrome** |
| **Leading** (more vulnerable) | Radial → ulnar deviation | Hamate stress injury, **hook of hamate #**, **ECU synovitis/subluxation**, TFCC |

**Dorsal rim impaction (TO)** — repetitive **axial load in dorsiflexion** → pinpoint radiocarpal pain on **loaded extension**; MRI: synovitis ± loose bodies; Rx training modification, strengthening, brace limiting dorsiflexion → arthroscopy if fails\
✗ Wrong options: passive

## 4 Diagnosis & Classification

### Key radiology findings

**Standard** — hand series + joint-specific views

**Pisotriquetral** — **supinated oblique, 30° supination** (profile of PT joint); **Garraud view** = 30° supination + slight extension

| View | Used for |
|---|---|
| **30° supinated oblique** | **Pisotriquetral arthritis** |
| Clenched-fist PA | **SL widening** |
| **Carpal tunnel view** | **Hook of hamate #** |
| Standard AP / lateral | not preferred for PT joint |

![Pisotriquetral arthritis](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob6056-pisotriquetrial-joint-arthritis.jpg)

## 5 Management

**First line** — **NSAIDs, bracing, intra-articular steroid**

### Radioscapholunate fusion – distal scaphoid & triquetrum excision

**Post-traumatic radiocarpal OA (after DRF)** — **RSL fusion + excise triquetrum + distal pole scaphoid** → frees midcarpal joint → ↑ radial deviation + F/E arc; alternative to total wrist fusion

(✗ Wrong options: **Traps** — AIN neurectomy (volar; fusion is dorsal)) · capitolunate interposition (capitolunate preserved) · dorsal intercarpal ligament section · ulnar shortening (unnecessary once triquetrum excised)

### Ulnar-sided wrist arthritis

**Pisotriquetral** — refractory → **pisiform excision**

| DRUJ arthrosis / abutment | Note |
|---|---|
| **Darrach** | Distal ulna resection |
| **Sauvé-Kapandji** | — |
| Partial resection + interposition | — |
| **Ulnar head replacement** | Primary or **salvage of failed Darrach**; early results promising |

![Ulnar head replacement](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob6056-ulnar-head-replacement.jpg)

## X vs Y

| | SLAC | SNAC |
|---|---|---|
| Cause | SL ligament tear | Scaphoid nonunion |
| First joint | Radiocarpal → capitolunate | Radial styloid–**distal** scaphoid |
| Radiolunate | **Spared** | **Spared** |

## How it's asked

**"Add to RSL fusion for post-DRF arthrosis?"** → **excise triquetrum + distal pole scaphoid**

**"Best image for pisotriquetral arthritis?"** → **30° supinated oblique view**\
✗ Wrong options: lateral · clenched fist · AP · carpal tunnel view

**"Trailing-wrist injury in golf?"** → **dorsal rim impaction syndrome** (TO)\
✗ Wrong options: ECU synovitis · hook of hamate · scaphoid #

**"Joint spared in SLAC/SNAC?"** → **radiolunate**

<div style="page-break-after: always;"></div>

# Wrist Arthroscopy

*Hand · 101 / 103*

## 1 Basics

### Indications

**Diagnostic + therapeutic** — **TFCC** · **SL / LT interosseous ligaments** · reduction aid (**distal radius, scaphoid**, perilunate (TO)) · **ulnocarpal impaction** / distal ulnar resection · chondral debridement · loose bodies · **synovectomy** · **dorsal ganglion** · SNAC/SLAC · **septic wrist I&D** · unexplained mechanical pain

### Set-up

**Position** — supine, **elbow 90°**, traction tower **10 lb** to fingers

**Landmarks** — Lister's tubercle, scaphoid, lunate, DRUJ, ECU

**Scope** — **2.7 mm, 30°**

**Portal creation** — **skin incision only → blunt (haemostat) dissection**

### Portals & nerves at risk

**Naming** — by adjacent **extensor compartments**; **≥11 dorsal**: 5 radiocarpal, 4 midcarpal, 2 DRUJ (TO: 9 radiocarpal/intercarpal + 2 DRUJ) + volar portal radial to FCR at proximal crease (TO)

| Radiocarpal portal | Location / use | At risk |
|---|---|---|
| **3-4** | **1 cm distal to Lister's**, EPL–EDC; **first portal, primary viewing ("workhorse")** | EPL, EDC; distal **PIN** (TO) |
| **4-5** | Ring MC line, EDC–EDM; instrumentation, **TFCC view** | EDC, EDM; distal PIN (TO) |
| **6R** | **Radial to ECU**; TFCC, UL/UT/LT ligaments, SLIL (TO) | **Dorsal sensory branch ulnar n.** (**~8 mm**) |
| **6U** | **Ulnar to ECU**; ulnar TFCC repair, irrigation | **Dorsal sensory branch ulnar n.** (± ulnar n./a.) |
| **1-2** | **APL–ECRB**, dorsal snuffbox; radial styloid, inflow, styloidectomy | **SBRN + radial artery** |

| Midcarpal / CMC portal | Location / use | At risk |
|---|---|---|
| **MCR** | 1 cm distal to 3-4, radial border middle MC, ECRB–EDC; **SL, SC, STT** joints | ECRB, EDC |
| **MCU** | 1 cm distal to 4-5, ring MC, EDC–EDM; **LC, LT, TH** joints | EDC, EDM |
| STT | Index MC axis, ulnar to EPL | ECRB, ECRL |
| TH (TO) | Ulnar, distal to triquetrum; inflow/outflow | — |
| **1U / 1R** | Ulnar / radial to **EPB** at thumb CMC; CMC DJD debridement | **SBRN** (portals straddle 1st branch); dorsal radial a. |

**Midcarpal portals** — needed for full carpal view & **instability** assessment

**SBRN distance from 3-4 — conflict** — **16 mm** (Orthobullets/OB) · **9 mm** closest branch (Kiliç cadaver study, OB) → superficial incision + blunt dissection

![Wrist portals](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob6009-wrist-portals.jpg)

![Portal map and nerves](../../MCQ/attachments/q4458-illa-wrsit-portals.jpg)

### Arthroscopic anatomy – visible structures

**Seen (radiocarpal)** — **SL & LT ligaments** · **superficial TFCC** over ulnar head · **radioscaphocapitate** (discrete volar capsular band)

**Not seen** — **ECU tendon** (extra-articular) · **dorsal intercarpal lig.** (dorsal)

**RSC ligament** — radial styloid → scaphoid waist → proximal radial capitate; **prevents ulnar translocation**; seen from 3-4 looking volar-radial

**Palmar oblique ligament** — 1st–2nd MC bases\
✗ Wrong options: wrist

![RSC from 3-4 portal](../../MCQ/attachments/q7791-a-3.jpg)

## 6 Complications

**Overall** — **1–2%**

**Most common overall (TO)** — **failure to achieve the procedure**

**Most common set-up (TO)** — finger traction → oedema, transient digital nerve neurapraxia

**Nerves** — **dorsal sensory ulnar** (6R/6U, esp. 6U for TFCC sutures → small open incision before knot tying) · **SBRN** (1-2, 1U, 1R) · **distal PIN** (3-4, 4-5; less mobile, 4th compartment floor) \
✗ Wrong options: **motor branch of ulnar n.** (TO)

**Dorsoradial wrist/thumb hyperaesthesia after scope** — **1-2 portal** (SBRN)

**Radial artery** — 1-2 portal (styloidectomy); dorsal branch with CMC portals

**Tendon injury — conflict** — **EDC** (index & small) most common (TO) · **EPL & EDM** from improper portal placement (Orthobullets); late **EPL rupture**, ECRL tenosynovitis (TO)

**Other** — chondral injury, portal infection, stiffness, **MCPJ pain (over-distraction)**

## Misfiled – kinematic TKA balancing

**Kinematic alignment (TO)** — restores **pre-arthritic** alignment & joint lines; **ligament-sparing** (bone cuts, no releases); femur positioned first, tibia adjusted

| KA imbalance (TO) | Correction |
|---|---|
| Tight extension & flexion | Remove more tibia |
| Tight extension, stable flexion | Remove posterior osteophytes, strip posterior capsule, ↓ posterior slope |
| Tight flexion, stable extension | ↑ posterior tibial slope |
| **Extension: tight medial, loose lateral** | **Remove medial osteophytes · recut tibia 2° more varus · thicker liner** (✗ Wrong options: **medial release**) |
| Extension: tight lateral, loose medial | Remove lateral osteophytes · recut 2° more valgus · thicker liner |
| Fixed valgus | Release arcuate complex + popliteus · recut 2° more varus |

## How it's asked

**"Most commonly injured tendon"** → **EDC** (TO)

**"Nerve injury NOT common"** → **motor branch of ulnar nerve** (TO)

**"Dorsoradial wrist/thumb hyperaesthesia after scope — portal?"** → **1-2**\
✗ Wrong options: 6U, 6R, MCU, 4-5

**"Initial portal"** → **3-4, 1 cm distal to Lister's**

**"Incorrect portal–nerve pair"** → **4-5 ; dorsal sensory ulnar**\
✗ Wrong options: correct: 1U/1-2 SBRN, 6U/6R DSBUN

**"Structure seen from 3-4 looking volar-radial"** → **radioscaphocapitate ligament**

**"Cannot be seen at radiocarpal arthroscopy"** → **extensor carpi ulnaris tendon**

**"KA TKA tight medial / loose lateral in extension — NOT recommended"** → **medial release** (TO)

<div style="page-break-after: always;"></div>

# Wrist Ligaments & Biomechanics

*Hand · 102 / 103*

## 1 Basics

### Wrist motion

**Joints** — radiocarpal + intercarpal; axes: flexion–extension, radial–ulnar deviation, prono-supination (TO)

| Motion | Normal | Functional | Contribution |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Flexion** | 65° | 10° | **40% radiocarpal / 60% midcarpal** |
| **Extension** | 55° | 35° | **66% radiocarpal / 33% midcarpal** |
| **Radial deviation** | 15° | 10° | **90% midcarpal** |
| Ulnar deviation | 35° | 15° | 50 / 50 |

### Carpal rows (TO)

**PCR (TO)** — scaphoid, lunate, triquetrum, pisiform; mobile between bones, moves as a row; **intercalated segment**\
✗ Wrong options: tendon attachments

**DCR (TO)** — trapezium, trapezoid, capitate, hamate; tightly bound → **single functional unit**

**Lunate–capitate (TO)** — **no ligamentous connection**

### Biomechanic concepts

| Concept | Key points |
|---|---|
| **Link** | Radius–lunate–capitate chain; **capitate head = centre of rotation**; ✓ efficient, strong volar ligaments; (✗ Wrong options: more links → instability)|
| **Column** | **Lateral (mobile)**: scaphoid, trapezium, trapezoid; **central (FE)**: lunate, capitate, hamate (lunocapitate = centre); **medial (rotation)**: triquetrum + distal row |
| **Row** | Proximal + distal rows; **scaphoid bridges** rows |
| Others (TO) | Row-column theory, **ring theory** |

**Scaphoid** — resting force / **radial deviation → scaphoid flexes**, triquetrum extends; **ulnar deviation → scaphoid extends**

![Link concept](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob6005-link-joint.jpg)

### Carpal relationships

| Ratio | Normal |
|---|---|
| **Carpal height / MC3 length** (collapse) | **0.54** |
| **Ulna–capitate / MC3** (ulnar translation) | **0.30** |

**Load** — **radius 80% / ulna 20%**; ulnar **lengthening ↑** ulnar load, **shortening ↓**

**Lunate alignment (PA)** — ≥ **2/3 of lunate** over radius (lunate fossa)

### Ligament overview

**Extrinsic** — bridge carpus to radius / metacarpals; volar + dorsal

**Intrinsic** — carpal-to-carpal; most important **SLIL + LTIL**

**Volar vs dorsal** — **volar stronger**; volar = secondary stabilisers of scaphoid; **dorsal converge on triquetrum**

**Space of Poirier** — weak area, **floor of carpal tunnel at proximal capitate**, between **RSC and long radiolunate**; ↑ in dorsiflexion, disappears in palmar flexion; perilunate: distal row separates from lunate · **lunate dislocation: lunate escapes into it**

![Space of Poirier](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob6005-space-of-poirier.jpg)

### Extrinsic ligaments – radioscaphocapitate & radiocarpal dislocation

| Ligament | Key fact |
|---|---|
| Radial collateral | Volar radiocarpal |
| **Radioscaphocapitate (RSC)** | Radial styloid → capitate; **sling for scaphoid waist**; **main restraint to ulnar translation**; **preserve in PRC** (primary stabiliser, prevents ulnar drift); at risk with large radial styloidectomy |
| **Long radiolunate** (radiolunotriquetral, volar RL) | Counteracts ulnar-distal lunate translocation; **abnormal in Madelung = Vickers ligament** |
| **Radioscapholunate (Testut & Kuentz)** | **Neurovascular conduit only**; no mechanical strength; mesocapsule with **AIN branches + radial arcade** rami (TO); extrinsic (TO) |
| **Short radiolunate** | Stabilises lunate; from **volar-ulnar "critical corner"** → **primary restraint to volar carpal translation**; disrupted in stage IV perilunate |
| Volar ulnocarpal | Ulnotriquetral, ulnolunate, ulnocapitate |
| **Dorsal radiotriquetral (DRC)** | Must be torn **with LTIL** for **VISI** |
| **Dorsal intercarpal (DIC)** | With DRC forms lateral **"V"** → secondary stabiliser vs **DISI** |
| Dorsal radiolunate, radioscaphoid | Dorsal extrinsic |

**Arthroscopy from 3-4 portal (EPL–EDC)** — volar extrinsics **radial → ulnar: RSC, long RL, short RL**; RSL (Testut) just radial to SLIL

![Arthroscopic volar extrinsic ligaments 1–3 radial to ulnar](../../MCQ/attachments/q5913-a-arthroscopic-volar-wrist-ligaments.jpg)

**Radiocarpal dislocation** — rare, high-energy; **dorsal > volar**; ORIF essential

| Ligament not repaired | Late deformity |
|---|---|
| **RSC** | **Ulnar translocation** of carpus (multidirectional instability) |
| **Short RL** (critical corner) | **Volar subluxation** of carpus |
| SLIL | DISI (Terry Thomas sign) |

**Surgical steps (Ilyas)** — provisional reduction → NV decompression → joint debridement → intercarpal injuries → fracture fixation / soft-tissue repair

**SL injury suspicion** — radial styloid fracture exiting at **scaphoid–lunate fossa junction**

**Classifications** — **Moneim** (intercarpal dissociation; type II & open → worse) · **Dumontier** (radial styloid involvement)

**RSC reconstruction** — **brachioradialis** tendon

### Intrinsic & extrinsic ligament function (LT, RSL)

| SLIL part | Role |
|---|---|
| **Dorsal** | **Thickest, strongest**; prevents **translation**; primary SL stabiliser |
| Volar | Prevents **rotation** |
| Proximal | No significant strength |

| LTIL part | Role |
|---|---|
| **Volar** | **Strongest** |
| Dorsal / proximal | Weaker |

**SLIL torn** — scaphoid flexes, lunate extends → **DISI**

**LTIL torn (+ dorsal radiotriquetral)** — lunate flexes with normal scaphoid → **VISI**; **volar LTIL** = most important contributor (TO)

**Isolated DRC or RSC injury (TO)** — no VISI

**Distal row** — trapeziotrapezoid, trapeziocapitate, capitohamate

**Palmar midcarpal** — STT, scaphocapitate, triquetrocapitate, triquetrohamate

![SLIL components](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob6005-slil.jpg)

### Secondary stabilisers against DISI – dorsal intercarpal ligament

**SL instability** — **most common carpal instability**; FOOSH

**Secondary SL stabilisers** — **DIC + DRC** (lateral V) → missed injury → persistent DISI → **SLAC**

**DIC** — assess and repair/reconstruct with SLIL surgery (Mitsuyasu)

**SLIL cut alone** — <2 mm translation; need SLIL + volar or dorsal ligaments for significant translation

![Dorsal intercarpal and dorsal radiocarpal ligaments](../../MCQ/attachments/q4595-illa-dia.jpg)

## X vs Y

| | **DISI** | **VISI** |
|---|---|---|
| Intrinsic | **SLIL (dorsal)** | **LTIL (volar)** |
| Extrinsic needed | DIC / DRC secondary | **Dorsal radiotriquetral** |
| Lunate | Extended | Flexed |
| Test | Watson scaphoid shift | Ballottement / shuck |

| Ligament | Classic MCQ association |
|---|---|
| RSC | PRC preservation · ulnar translocation |
| Long RL | Madelung (Vickers) |
| Short RL | Critical corner · volar subluxation · stage IV perilunate |
| RSL (Testut) | Neurovascular conduit |
| Dorsal radiotriquetral | VISI with LTIL |
| DIC | Secondary DISI stabiliser |

## How it's asked

**"Volar extrinsic disrupted → ulnar translation after radiocarpal dislocation?"** → **RSC**\
✗ Wrong options: short RL, SLIL, radiotriquetral, RSL

**"Arthroscopic structures 1, 2, 3 from 3-4 portal?"** → **RSC, long RL, short RL**

**"RSC repaired — without surgery what deformity?"** → **ulnar translocation of carpus** (Figure D)\
✗ Wrong options: volar subluxation = short RL · SLIL · perilunate · Madelung

**"PRC — preserve which ligament?"** → **RSC**

**"Secondary stabiliser preventing DISI?"** → **dorsal intercarpal ligament**

**"VISI, positive ballottement — ligament?"** → **volar lunotriquetral interosseous** (TO)

**"RSL all except?"** → **provides mechanical strength** — false (TO)

**"Ligament abnormal in Madelung?"** → **long radiolunate (Vickers)**

**"Lunate escapes into?"** → **space of Poirier**

**"Radial deviation is mostly?"** → **midcarpal (90%)**

<div style="page-break-after: always;"></div>

# Wrist Trauma Radiographic Evaluation

*Hand · 103 / 103*

## 1 Basics

### Carpal ossification sequence

**Order** — counterclockwise (right hand) from capitate: **capitate → hamate → triquetrum → lunate → scaphoid → trapezium → trapezoid → pisiform**

**First** — **capitate** (largest carpal bone), **1–3 months**, often with hamate

**Last** — **pisiform** (only **sesamoid** carpal bone)

| Bone | Ossifies (Orthobullets table) | OB explanation | Fusion |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Capitate** | **1 y** | **1–3 mo** | 14–16 y |
| Hamate | 2 y | shortly after capitate | 14–16 y |
| Triquetrum | 3 y | — | 14–16 y |
| Lunate (Kienböck) | 4 y | ~5 y | 14–16 y |
| Scaphoid (Preiser) | 5 y | ~6 y | 14–16 y |
| Trapezium (thumb MC base) | 5–6 y | ~6 y | 14–16 y |
| Trapezoid | 6–7 y | — | 14–16 y |
| **Pisiform** | **9–10 y** | ~12 y | 14–16 y |

**Conflict** — ages differ between sources (above) — read the options; order is the same

![Carpal ossification order](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob322143-carpal-ossification-order.jpg)

### Gilula's arcs

| Arc | Line |
|---|---|
| Proximal | Proximal scaphoid + lunate + triquetrum |
| Middle | Distal scaphoid + lunate + triquetrum |
| Distal | Proximal capitate + hamate |

![Gilula's carpal arcs](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob322143-carpal-arcs.jpg)

## 4 Diagnosis & Classification

### Amsterdam wrist rules (X-ray criteria)

| X-ray if any | Suspected distal radius # | Suspected carpal # |
|---|---|---|
| ↑ age, swelling, deformity, TTP distal radius | Pain on **palmarflexion**, **supination**, painful **radioulnar ballottement**; pain on ulnar deviation = ↓ risk | **Male**, **snuffbox swelling**, pain on **radial deviation**; painful thumb axial compression = ↓ risk |

### Key radiology findings – views

| View | Position / beam | Best for |
|---|---|---|
| **AP / PA** | Shoulder abd 90°, elbow 90°; supinated (AP) / pronated (PA) | **Carpal joints: AP > PA**; DRF |
| **Lateral** | **Shoulder adducted**, elbow 90°, forearm neutral; 10° cephalad | Alignment, carpal instability; adequate = pisiform volar cortex within central 1/3 of volar scaphoid |
| **IR oblique** (pronated 45°) | | **Radial carpus**; **best for scaphoid waist + distal pole** |
| **ER oblique** (supinated 45°) | | **Ulnar carpus**; **pisiform, hamate body** |
| Ulnar deviation | Aim at scaphoid | Scaphoid #, lateral wrist; scaphoid/lunate **extend** |
| Radial deviation | | Medial wrist, palmar triquetral #; scaphoid/lunate **flex** |
| **Carpal tunnel** | Wrist extended; 2.5 cm distal to MC3 base, 25–30° cephalad | **Hook of hamate (best)**, pisiform, trapezium |
| Carpal bridge | 4 cm proximal to wrist bend, 45° caudad | Pisiform, trapezium, hamate hook |
| **Clenched fist** | Supinated, ulnar dev, fist (capitate driven proximally); bilateral | **SL injury** |
| **Scaphoid view** | **30° extension + 20° ulnar deviation** | Scaphoid # |
| **Clements-Nakayama** | Pronated 45°, ulnar dev; at snuffbox (20° cephalad if can't deviate) | **Trapezium #** |

**Why adduct shoulder for lateral** — ulna does not rotate with pronation/supination

### Key radiology findings – signs

| Sign | View | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| **Pronator quadratus fat pad** | Lateral | Wrist effusion (bowed PQ fascia) |
| Scaphoid fat pad | Ulnar deviation | Effusion / scaphoid # |
| **Piece-of-pie** (triangular lunate) | PA | Lunate dislocation |
| **Spilled teacup** | Lateral | **Stage IV**: lunate volar to radius |
| **Pooping duck** | Lateral / IR oblique | **Dorsal triquetral cortical #** |
| **Absent "eye" (cortical ring)** + sclerosis | PA | **Hook of hamate #** |

### Scaphoid fracture

| Location | % |
|---|---|
| **Waist** | **65** |
| Proximal 1/3 | 25 |
| Distal 1/3 | 10 (**commonest in children**) |

**PA** — proximal pole; waist/distal pole distorted (scaphoid flexed)

**Negative X-ray + high suspicion** — thumb spica, **repeat X-ray 14–21 d** (re-evaluate 12–21 d)

| ORIF criteria (instability) | Threshold |
|---|---|
| Displacement | **>1 mm** any view |
| **Humpback** | **15°** |
| SL angle | **>60°** |
| Radiolunate angle (DISI) | **>15°** |
| **Intrascaphoid angle** | **>35°** |
| Other | Proximal pole, perilunate, comminuted, vertical/oblique unstable, non-displaced waist (listed) |

### Lunate / perilunate dislocation

**PA** — **break in Gilula's arc**, lunate–capitate overlap, **piece-of-pie**

**Lateral** — **loss of radius–lunate–capitate colinearity**; stage III capitate dorsal to lunate; **stage IV spilled teacup**; SL angle **>70°**

| Timing | Treatment |
|---|---|
| Acute **<8 wk** | Emergent closed reduction/splint → **open reduction, ligament repair, fixation ± CTR** (✗ Wrong options: nonop) |
| Chronic **>8 wk** | **PRC** |
| Chronic + degenerative | **Total wrist arthrodesis** |

![Piece-of-pie sign: triangular lunate](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob322143-piece-of-pie-sign-with-triangular-lunate.jpg)

![Spilled teacup sign](../../Orthobullets/attachments/ob322143-spilled-teacup-sign.jpg)

### Other carpal fractures

| Fracture | Best view | Nonop | Surgery |
|---|---|---|---|
| Triquetrum | Lateral / IR oblique (dorsal); radial dev (palmar) | Stable dorsal/palmar cortical, undisplaced body | ORIF if unstable / displaced body |
| **Hook of hamate** | **Carpal tunnel** | Most | **Excise chronic nonunion** |
| Hamate body | **ER oblique** | Extra-articular undisplaced (rare) | ORIF displaced / intra-articular |
| Pisiform | **ER oblique or carpal tunnel** | Most | **Pisiformectomy**: displaced symptomatic, painful nonunion |
| Trapezium | Clements-Nakayama, carpal tunnel | | |

### Normal variants (accessory ossicles)

| Ossicle | Location |
|---|---|
| Lunula | In TFCC between ulnar styloid + triquetrum |
| Os triangulare | Ulnar styloid + lunate + triquetrum |
| **Os hamuli proprium** | Unfused hook of hamate ossification centre (mimics #) |
| Os centrale carpi | Dorsal, scaphoid + trapezoid + capitate |
| Os epilunate | Dorsal lunocapitate joint |
| Os styloideum | Between MC2 & MC3 bases |
| Os paratrapezium | Radius side / base of MC1 |
| Os radiostyloideum | Near radial styloid, lateral to mid-scaphoid |

## How it's asked

**"Ossification centre appears at 1–3 months?"** → **largest carpal bone = capitate**\
✗ Wrong options: scaphoid/Preiser, lunate/Kienböck, trapezium, pisiform

**"Last carpal bone to ossify?"** → **pisiform**

**"Best view for hook of hamate?"** → **carpal tunnel view**

**"Best view for scaphoid waist + distal pole?"** → **IR (pronated) oblique**

**"Scaphoid view position?"** → **30° extension + 20° ulnar deviation**

**"Suspected scaphoid #, normal X-ray?"** → **thumb spica, repeat X-ray 14–21 d**

**"Stage IV perilunate lateral sign?"** → **spilled teacup**

**"Pooping duck sign?"** → **dorsal triquetral cortical fracture**
